2026-10-12T07:38:29.3662927Z  	 SELECT wi.*, wv.version_id AS original_version_id, wd.name AS original_name INTO STRICT i

2026-10-12T07:38:29.3664441Z  	 WHERE wi.app_id=NEW.app_id AND wi.id=NEW.instance_id;
2026-10-12T07:38:29.3664581Z  	 IF (c->>'ProtocolVersion')::integer IS DISTINCT FROM 2
2026-10-12T07:38:29.3664729Z  	 OR (c->>'CommandID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.command_id
2026-10-12T07:38:29.3664856Z  	 OR (c->>'AppID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.app_id

2026-10-12T07:38:29.3665820Z  	 OR (c->>'TableID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.table_id
2026-10-12T07:38:29.3665943Z  	 OR (c->>'ViewID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM i.view_id

2026-10-12T07:38:29.3945540Z  	 IF NOT FOUND THEN

2026-10-12T07:38:29.3946457Z  	   RAISE EXCEPTION 'publication history scope mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503';
